========== tests/CombatQuickTest.cpp ==========

#include "../src/systems/CombatSystem.h"
#include "../src/systems/CollisionSystem.h"
#include "../src/systems/GameManager.h"
#include "../src/entities/MeleePlayer.h"
#include "../src/entities/RangedPlayer.h"
#include "../src/entities/DefensePlayer.h"
#include "../src/entities/EasyEnemy.h"
#include "../src/entities/HardEnemy.h"
#include "../src/entities/EnemyProjectile.h"

#include <iostream>
#include <memory>

int passed = 0;
int failed = 0;

void check(bool condition, const char* name) {
    std::cout << (condition ? "[PASS] " : "[FAIL] ")
              << name << '\n';

    if (condition)
        ++passed;
    else
        ++failed;
}

struct TestGame {
    GameManager manager;
    CollisionSystem collision;
    CombatSystem combat{&manager, &collision};

    template<typename T>
    T* addPlayer(float x = 0.30f, int lane = 0, int hp = 5) {
        auto player = std::make_unique<T>(
            x, 0.40f, 0.05f, 0.07f, lane, hp, 0.0f
        );

        T* result = player.get();
        result->setActionState(ActionState::Moving);

        manager.players.push_back(std::move(player));
        return result;
    }
};

int main() {

    // TEST 1: Melee damages enemy
    {
        TestGame game;
        game.addPlayer<MeleePlayer>();

        HardEnemy enemy(0, {0.34f, 0.40f}, 2.0f);

        game.combat.update(0.0f, {&enemy});

        check(enemy.getHealth() == 6,
              "Melee player damages enemy");
    }

    // TEST 2: Ranged player creates a bullet
    {
        TestGame game;
        game.addPlayer<RangedPlayer>();

        game.combat.resolvePlayerAttacks({});

        check(game.manager.getActiveProjectiles().size() == 1,
              "Ranged player shoots automatically");
    }

    // TEST 3: Player bullet damages enemy once
    {
        TestGame game;
        auto* ranged = game.addPlayer<RangedPlayer>();

        game.manager.addProjectile(
            std::make_unique<Projectile>(
                0.41f, 0.42f, 0.012f, 0.012f,
                0, 0.5f, ranged
            )
        );

        EasyEnemy enemy(0, {0.40f, 0.40f}, 2.0f);

        game.combat.resolvePlayerProjectiles({&enemy});
        const int afterFirstHit = enemy.getHealth();

        game.combat.resolvePlayerProjectiles({&enemy});

        check(afterFirstHit == 4,
              "Player bullet deals 1 damage");

        check(enemy.getHealth() == afterFirstHit,
              "Player bullet cannot hit twice");
    }

    // TEST 4: High attack damages standing player
    {
        TestGame game;
        auto* player = game.addPlayer<MeleePlayer>();

        EnemyProjectile shot(
            0.31f, 0.42f, 0, 0.15f, 1, AttackType::High
        );

        game.combat.resolveEnemyProjectiles({&shot});

        check(player->getHealth() == 4 && !shot.isActive(),
              "High bullet damages standing player once");
    }

    // TEST 5: Duck avoids high attack
    {
        TestGame game;
        auto* player = game.addPlayer<MeleePlayer>();

        player->duck();

        EnemyProjectile shot(
            0.31f, 0.42f, 0, 0.15f, 1, AttackType::High
        );

        game.combat.resolveEnemyProjectiles({&shot});

        check(player->getHealth() == 5,
              "Ducking avoids high bullet");
    }

    // TEST 6: Jump avoids low attack
    {
        TestGame game;
        auto* player = game.addPlayer<MeleePlayer>();

        player->jump();

        EnemyProjectile shot(
            0.31f, 0.42f, 0, 0.15f, 1, AttackType::Low
        );

        game.combat.resolveEnemyProjectiles({&shot});

        check(player->getHealth() == 5,
              "Jumping avoids low bullet");
    }

    // TEST 7: Far enemy cannot cause instant damage
    {
        TestGame game;
        auto* player = game.addPlayer<MeleePlayer>();

        EasyEnemy enemy(0, {0.80f, 0.40f}, 2.0f);

        game.combat.resolveEnemyAttacks({&enemy});

        check(player->getHealth() == 5,
              "Far enemy cannot damage without bullet collision");
    }

    // TEST 8: Defense absorbs enemy bullet
    {
        TestGame game;

        auto* player = game.addPlayer<MeleePlayer>(0.30f);
        auto* defense = game.addPlayer<DefensePlayer>(0.50f, 0, 7);

        EnemyProjectile shot(
            0.31f, 0.42f, 0, 0.15f, 1, AttackType::High
        );

        game.combat.resolveEnemyProjectiles({&shot});

        check(player->getHealth() == 5 &&
              defense->getHealth() == 6,
              "Defense absorbs damage for protected player");
    }

    // TEST 9: Defense absorbs melee damage
    {
        TestGame game;

        auto* melee = game.addPlayer<MeleePlayer>(0.30f);
        auto* defense = game.addPlayer<DefensePlayer>(0.50f, 0, 7);

        HardEnemy enemy(0, {0.34f, 0.40f}, 2.0f);

        game.combat.resolveEnemyAttacks({&enemy});

        check(melee->getHealth() == 5 &&
              defense->getHealth() == 5 &&
              !enemy.canAttack(),
              "Defense absorbs melee damage");
    }

    // TEST 10: Collision blocks movement during cooldown
    {
        TestGame game;

        auto* player = game.addPlayer<MeleePlayer>(0.30f);
        player->setActionState(ActionState::Attacking);

        EasyEnemy enemy(0, {0.34f, 0.40f}, 2.0f);

        game.combat.update(0.0f, {&enemy});

        check(player->isMovementBlocked() &&
              enemy.isMovementBlocked(),
              "Collision blocks movement during attack cooldown");
    }

    // TEST 11: Bullet cannot hit a player in another lane
    {
        TestGame game;

        auto* player = game.addPlayer<MeleePlayer>(0.30f, 0, 5);

        EnemyProjectile shot(
            0.31f, 0.42f, 1, 0.15f, 1, AttackType::High
        );

        game.combat.resolveEnemyProjectiles({&shot});

        check(player->getHealth() == 5 && shot.isActive(),
              "Bullet cannot hit player in another lane");
    }

    // TEST 12: One bullet damages only one enemy
    {
        TestGame game;

        auto* ranged = game.addPlayer<RangedPlayer>();

        game.manager.addProjectile(
            std::make_unique<Projectile>(
                0.41f, 0.42f, 0.012f, 0.012f,
                0, 0.5f, ranged
            )
        );

        EasyEnemy enemy1(0, {0.40f, 0.40f}, 2.0f);
        EasyEnemy enemy2(0, {0.40f, 0.40f}, 2.0f);

        game.combat.resolvePlayerProjectiles({&enemy1, &enemy2});

        check(enemy1.getHealth() == 4 &&
              enemy2.getHealth() == 5,
              "One bullet damages only one enemy");
    }

    // TEST 13: One enemy bullet damages only one player
    {
        TestGame game;

        auto* player1 = game.addPlayer<MeleePlayer>(0.30f);
        auto* player2 = game.addPlayer<MeleePlayer>(0.30f);

        EnemyProjectile shot(
            0.31f, 0.42f, 0, 0.15f, 1, AttackType::High
        );

        game.combat.resolveEnemyProjectiles({&shot});

        check(player1->getHealth() == 4 &&
              player2->getHealth() == 5 &&
              !shot.isActive(),
              "One enemy bullet damages only one player");
    }

    // TEST 14: Inactive enemy bullet does not damage player
    {
        TestGame game;

        auto* player = game.addPlayer<MeleePlayer>();

        EnemyProjectile shot(
            0.31f, 0.42f, 0, 0.15f, 1, AttackType::High
        );

        shot.setActive(false);

        game.combat.resolveEnemyProjectiles({&shot});

        check(player->getHealth() == 5,
              "Inactive enemy bullet causes no damage");
    }

    // TEST 15: Enemy bullet misses distant player
    {
        TestGame game;

        auto* player = game.addPlayer<MeleePlayer>();

        EnemyProjectile shot(
            0.80f, 0.42f, 0, 0.15f, 1, AttackType::High
        );

        game.combat.resolveEnemyProjectiles({&shot});

        check(player->getHealth() == 5 &&
              shot.isActive(),
              "Enemy bullet misses distant player");
    }

    // TEST 16: Player bullet cannot damage enemy in another lane
    {
        TestGame game;

        auto* ranged = game.addPlayer<RangedPlayer>();

        game.manager.addProjectile(
            std::make_unique<Projectile>(
                0.41f, 0.42f, 0.012f, 0.012f,
                0, 0.5f, ranged
            )
        );

        EasyEnemy enemy(1, {0.40f, 0.40f}, 2.0f);

        game.combat.resolvePlayerProjectiles({&enemy});

        check(enemy.getHealth() == 5,
              "Player bullet cannot damage enemy in another lane");
    }

    // TEST 17: Player bullet misses distant enemy
    {
        TestGame game;

        auto* ranged = game.addPlayer<RangedPlayer>();

        game.manager.addProjectile(
            std::make_unique<Projectile>(
                0.41f, 0.42f, 0.012f, 0.012f,
                0, 0.5f, ranged
            )
        );

        EasyEnemy enemy(0, {0.80f, 0.40f}, 2.0f);

        game.combat.resolvePlayerProjectiles({&enemy});

        check(enemy.getHealth() == 5,
              "Player bullet misses distant enemy");
    }

    // TEST 18: Melee player cannot hit enemy in another lane
    {
        TestGame game;

        game.addPlayer<MeleePlayer>(0.30f, 0);

        EasyEnemy enemy(1, {0.34f, 0.40f}, 2.0f);

        game.combat.resolvePlayerAttacks({&enemy});

        check(enemy.getHealth() == 5,
              "Melee player cannot hit enemy in another lane");
    }

    // TEST 19: Melee player cannot hit distant enemy
    {
        TestGame game;

        game.addPlayer<MeleePlayer>(0.30f);

        EasyEnemy enemy(0, {0.80f, 0.40f}, 2.0f);

        game.combat.resolvePlayerAttacks({&enemy});

        check(enemy.getHealth() == 5,
              "Melee player cannot hit distant enemy");
    }

    // TEST 20: One melee attack damages only one enemy
    {
        TestGame game;

        game.addPlayer<MeleePlayer>(0.30f);

        EasyEnemy enemy1(0, {0.34f, 0.40f}, 2.0f);
        EasyEnemy enemy2(0, {0.34f, 0.40f}, 2.0f);

        game.combat.resolvePlayerAttacks({&enemy1, &enemy2});

        check(enemy1.getHealth() == 4 &&
              enemy2.getHealth() == 5,
              "One melee attack damages only one enemy");
    }

    // TEST 21: Enemy cannot attack twice during cooldown
    {
        TestGame game;

        auto* player = game.addPlayer<MeleePlayer>(0.30f);

        HardEnemy enemy(0, {0.34f, 0.40f}, 2.0f);

        game.combat.resolveEnemyAttacks({&enemy});
        game.combat.resolveEnemyAttacks({&enemy});

        check(player->getHealth() == 3 &&
              !enemy.canAttack(),
              "Enemy cannot attack twice during cooldown");
    }

    // TEST 22: Defender cannot protect player in another lane
    {
        TestGame game;

        auto* player = game.addPlayer<MeleePlayer>(0.30f, 0, 5);
        auto* defense = game.addPlayer<DefensePlayer>(0.50f, 1, 7);

        EnemyProjectile shot(
            0.31f, 0.42f, 0, 0.15f, 1, AttackType::High
        );

        game.combat.resolveEnemyProjectiles({&shot});

        check(player->getHealth() == 4 &&
              defense->getHealth() == 7,
              "Defender cannot protect another lane");
    }

    // TEST 23: Disabled defense cannot absorb damage
    {
        TestGame game;

        auto* player = game.addPlayer<MeleePlayer>(0.30f);
        auto* defense = game.addPlayer<DefensePlayer>(0.50f, 0, 7);

        defense->disableProtection();

        EnemyProjectile shot(
            0.31f, 0.42f, 0, 0.15f, 1, AttackType::High
        );

        game.combat.resolveEnemyProjectiles({&shot});

        check(player->getHealth() == 4 &&
              defense->getHealth() == 7,
              "Disabled defender cannot absorb damage");
    }

    // TEST 24: No collision means no movement blocking
    {
        TestGame game;

        auto* player = game.addPlayer<MeleePlayer>(0.30f);

        EasyEnemy enemy(0, {0.80f, 0.40f}, 2.0f);

        game.combat.update(0.0f, {&enemy});

        check(!player->isMovementBlocked() &&
              !enemy.isMovementBlocked(),
              "No collision means movement is not blocked");
    }


    std::cout << "\nPassed: " << passed
              << " | Failed: " << failed << '\n';

    return failed == 0 ? 0 : 1;
}

========== tests/EnemyTest.cpp ==========

#include <iostream>
#include <cmath>
#include <string>
#include <memory>
#include <limits>
#include "../src/utils/Constants.h"
#include "Enemy.h"
#include "EasyEnemy.h"
#include "MediumEnemy.h"
#include "HardEnemy.h"

int passed = 0;
int failed = 0;

// Prints whether each test passed or failed
void check(bool condition, const std::string& testName) {
    if (condition) {
        std::cout << "[PASS] " << testName << '\n';
        passed++;
    } else {
        std::cout << "[FAIL] " << testName << '\n';
        failed++;
    }
}

// Compare decimal numbers safely
bool approximatelyEqual(float a, float b) {
    return std::fabs(a - b) < 0.001f;
}

int main() {

    std::cout << "===== ENEMY CLASS TESTS =====\n\n";

    // -----------------------------------------
    // TEST 1: Constructor and starting values
    // -----------------------------------------

    Enemy enemy(5, 1, 60.0f, 2.0f, 2,
                glm::vec2(900.0f, 250.0f));

    check(enemy.getHealth() == 5,
          "Initial health is 5");

    check(enemy.getMaxHealth() == 5,
          "Maximum health is 5");

    check(enemy.getDamage() == 1,
          "Damage is 1");

    check(approximatelyEqual(enemy.getSpeed(), 60.0f),
          "Speed is 60");

    check(enemy.getLane() == 2,
          "Enemy starts in lane 2");

    check(enemy.getState() == EnemyState::MOVING,
          "Enemy starts in MOVING state");

    check(approximatelyEqual(enemy.getPosition().x, 900.0f),
          "Starting X is 900");

    check(approximatelyEqual(enemy.getPosition().y, 250.0f),
          "Starting Y is 250");

    // -----------------------------------------
    // TEST 2: Movement
    // -----------------------------------------

    std::cout << "\n--- Movement Tests ---\n";

    enemy.update(0.5f);

    check(approximatelyEqual(enemy.getPosition().x, 870.0f),
          "After 0.5 seconds, X is 870");

    enemy.update(0.5f);

    check(approximatelyEqual(enemy.getPosition().x, 840.0f),
          "After 1 total second, X is 840");

    check(approximatelyEqual(enemy.getPosition().y, 250.0f),
          "Y does not change during movement");

    // -----------------------------------------
    // TEST 3: Fighting state
    // -----------------------------------------

    std::cout << "\n--- State Tests ---\n";

    enemy.setState(EnemyState::FIGHTING);

    check(enemy.getState() == EnemyState::FIGHTING,
          "Enemy changes to FIGHTING");

    enemy.update(1.0f);

    check(approximatelyEqual(enemy.getPosition().x, 840.0f),
          "Enemy does not move while FIGHTING");

    // -----------------------------------------
    // TEST 4: Attack cooldown
    // -----------------------------------------

    std::cout << "\n--- Attack Timer Tests ---\n";

    // Total elapsed time: 0.5 + 0.5 + 1.0 = 2.0
    check(enemy.canAttack(),
          "Enemy can attack after 2 seconds");

    enemy.resetAttackTimer();

    check(!enemy.canAttack(),
          "Enemy cannot attack immediately after reset");

    enemy.update(0.5f);

    check(!enemy.canAttack(),
          "Enemy cannot attack after only 0.5 seconds");

    check(approximatelyEqual(enemy.getTimeSinceLastAttack(), 0.5f),
          "Attack timer increased to 0.5");

    enemy.update(1.5f);

    check(enemy.canAttack(),
          "Enemy can attack when cooldown is complete");

    // -----------------------------------------
    // TEST 5: Return to movement
    // -----------------------------------------

    std::cout << "\n--- Resume Movement Test ---\n";

    enemy.setState(EnemyState::MOVING);
    enemy.update(1.0f);

    check(approximatelyEqual(enemy.getPosition().x, 780.0f),
          "Enemy resumes moving after FIGHTING");

    // -----------------------------------------
    // TEST 6: Taking damage
    // -----------------------------------------

    std::cout << "\n--- Damage Tests ---\n";

    enemy.takeDamage(2);

    check(enemy.getHealth() == 3,
          "Taking 2 damage reduces health to 3");

    enemy.takeDamage(-5);

    check(enemy.getHealth() == 3,
          "Negative damage is ignored");

    enemy.takeDamage(10);

    check(enemy.getHealth() == 0,
          "Health cannot become negative");

    check(enemy.getState() == EnemyState::DEAD,
          "Enemy enters DEAD state at zero health");

    // -----------------------------------------
    // TEST 7: Dead enemies
    // -----------------------------------------

    std::cout << "\n--- Death Tests ---\n";

    float previousX = enemy.getPosition().x;
    float previousTimer = enemy.getTimeSinceLastAttack();

    enemy.update(2.0f);

    check(approximatelyEqual(enemy.getPosition().x, previousX),
          "Dead enemy cannot move");

    check(approximatelyEqual(
              enemy.getTimeSinceLastAttack(), previousTimer),
          "Dead enemy's attack timer stops");

    check(!enemy.canAttack(),
          "Dead enemy cannot attack");

    enemy.setState(EnemyState::MOVING);

    check(enemy.getState() == EnemyState::DEAD,
          "Dead enemy cannot return to MOVING");

    enemy.takeDamage(5);

    check(enemy.getHealth() == 0,
          "Dead enemy ignores further damage");

    // -----------------------------------------
    // TEST 8: Prevent unauthorized death
    // -----------------------------------------

    std::cout << "\n--- State Protection Test ---\n";

    Enemy healthyEnemy(5, 1, 60.0f, 2.0f, 0,
                       glm::vec2(900.0f, 100.0f));

    healthyEnemy.setState(EnemyState::DEAD);

    check(healthyEnemy.getState() == EnemyState::MOVING,
          "setState cannot kill a healthy enemy");

    check(healthyEnemy.getHealth() == 5,
          "Healthy enemy retains its health");
    
    
    // -----------------------------------------
    // TEST 9: EasyEnemy (Sword Fighter)
    // -----------------------------------------

    std::cout << "\n--- EasyEnemy Tests ---\n";

    EasyEnemy swordFighter(
        2,
        glm::vec2(900.0f, 250.0f),
        2.0f
    );

    // Test initial properties
    check(swordFighter.getHealth() == 5,
          "EasyEnemy starts with 5 HP");

    check(swordFighter.getMaxHealth() == 5,
          "EasyEnemy maximum health is 5");

    check(swordFighter.getDamage() == 1,
          "EasyEnemy deals 1 damage");

    check(approximatelyEqual(swordFighter.getSpeed(), 60.0f),
          "EasyEnemy speed is 60");

    check(swordFighter.getLane() == 2,
          "EasyEnemy spawns in the correct lane");

    check(swordFighter.getState() == EnemyState::MOVING,
          "EasyEnemy starts in MOVING state");

    check(approximatelyEqual(swordFighter.getAttackInterval(), 2.0f),
          "EasyEnemy uses the supplied attack interval");

    // Test inherited movement
    swordFighter.update(0.5f);

    check(approximatelyEqual(swordFighter.getPosition().x, 870.0f),
          "EasyEnemy inherits movement correctly");

    check(approximatelyEqual(swordFighter.getPosition().y, 250.0f),
          "EasyEnemy stays in its lane");

    // Test inherited damage
    swordFighter.takeDamage(2);

    check(swordFighter.getHealth() == 3,
          "EasyEnemy inherits damage handling");

    // Test inherited death behavior
    swordFighter.takeDamage(5);

    check(swordFighter.getHealth() == 0,
          "EasyEnemy health reaches zero");

    check(swordFighter.getState() == EnemyState::DEAD,
          "EasyEnemy dies correctly");

    check(!swordFighter.canAttack(),
          "Dead EasyEnemy cannot attack");


    
    // -----------------------------------------
    // TEST 10: MediumEnemy (Archer)
    // -----------------------------------------

    std::cout << "\n--- MediumEnemy Tests ---\n";

    MediumEnemy archer(
        1,
        glm::vec2(900.0f, 175.0f),
        1.5f
    );

    // Test initial properties
    check(archer.getHealth() == 5,
          "MediumEnemy starts with 5 HP");

    check(archer.getMaxHealth() == 5,
          "MediumEnemy maximum health is 5");

    check(archer.getDamage() == 1,
          "MediumEnemy deals 1 damage");

    check(approximatelyEqual(archer.getSpeed(), 60.0f),
          "MediumEnemy speed is 60");

    check(archer.getLane() == 1,
          "MediumEnemy spawns in the correct lane");

    check(archer.getState() == EnemyState::MOVING,
          "MediumEnemy starts in MOVING state");

    check(approximatelyEqual(archer.getAttackInterval(), 1.5f),
          "MediumEnemy uses the supplied attack interval");

    // Test inherited movement
    archer.update(0.5f);

    check(approximatelyEqual(archer.getPosition().x, 870.0f),
          "MediumEnemy inherits movement correctly");

    check(approximatelyEqual(archer.getPosition().y, 175.0f),
          "MediumEnemy stays in its lane");

    // Test inherited damage
    archer.takeDamage(2);

    check(archer.getHealth() == 3,
          "MediumEnemy inherits damage handling");

    // Test inherited death behavior
    archer.takeDamage(5);

    check(archer.getHealth() == 0,
          "MediumEnemy health reaches zero");

    check(archer.getState() == EnemyState::DEAD,
          "MediumEnemy dies correctly");

    check(!archer.canAttack(),
          "Dead MediumEnemy cannot attack");

      
    // -----------------------------------------
    // TEST 11: HardEnemy (Armored Knight)
    // -----------------------------------------

    std::cout << "\n--- HardEnemy Tests ---\n";

    HardEnemy knight(
        3,
        glm::vec2(900.0f, 325.0f),
        2.0f
    );

    check(knight.getHealth() == 7,
          "HardEnemy starts with 7 HP");

    check(knight.getMaxHealth() == 7,
          "HardEnemy maximum health is 7");

    check(knight.getDamage() == 2,
          "HardEnemy deals 2 damage");

    check(approximatelyEqual(knight.getSpeed(), 110.0f),
          "HardEnemy speed is 110");

    check(knight.getLane() == 3,
          "HardEnemy spawns in the correct lane");

    check(knight.getState() == EnemyState::MOVING,
          "HardEnemy starts in MOVING state");

    check(approximatelyEqual(knight.getAttackInterval(), 2.0f),
          "HardEnemy uses the supplied attack interval");

    // Inherited movement
    knight.update(0.5f);

    check(approximatelyEqual(knight.getPosition().x, 845.0f),
          "HardEnemy moves 55 units in 0.5 seconds");

    check(approximatelyEqual(knight.getPosition().y, 325.0f),
          "HardEnemy stays in its lane");

    // Inherited damage handling
    knight.takeDamage(2);

    check(knight.getHealth() == 5,
          "HardEnemy health decreases from 7 to 5");

    // Inherited death behavior
    knight.takeDamage(10);

    check(knight.getHealth() == 0,
          "HardEnemy health cannot become negative");

    check(knight.getState() == EnemyState::DEAD,
          "HardEnemy enters DEAD state");

    check(!knight.canAttack(),
          "Dead HardEnemy cannot attack");

    // -----------------------------------------
    // ADDITIONAL BOUNDARY AND EDGE CASES
    // -----------------------------------------
    
std::cout << "\n--- Initial Values and Zero Time ---\n";

Enemy initial(5, 2, 60.0f, 2.0f, 3,
              glm::vec2(900.0f, 325.0f));

check(approximatelyEqual(initial.getTimeSinceLastAttack(),
                         initial.getAttackInterval()),
      "Attack timer starts at the cooldown threshold");

check(approximatelyEqual(initial.getAttackInterval(), 2.0f),
      "Base enemy preserves its attack interval");

check(initial.canAttack(),
      "Fresh enemy can attack immediately");

float timerBefore = initial.getTimeSinceLastAttack();

initial.update(0.0f);

check(approximatelyEqual(initial.getPosition().x, 900.0f),
      "Zero deltaTime does not move enemy");

check(approximatelyEqual(initial.getTimeSinceLastAttack(), timerBefore),
      "Zero deltaTime does not advance cooldown");

    std::cout << "\n--- Cooldown Thresholds ---\n";
    Enemy cooldown(5, 1, 60.0f, 2.0f, 0,
               glm::vec2(900.0f, 100.0f));

// Simulate the first attack, starting the next cooldown
cooldown.resetAttackTimer();

cooldown.update(1.5f);
    check(!cooldown.canAttack(), "Cannot attack strictly before cooldown ends");
    cooldown.update(0.5f);
    check(cooldown.canAttack(), "Can attack exactly at cooldown threshold");
    check(approximatelyEqual(cooldown.getTimeSinceLastAttack(), 2.0f),
          "Cooldown timer is exactly 2 seconds at threshold");
    cooldown.update(4.0f);
    check(cooldown.canAttack(), "Cooldown remains ready until manually reset");
    check(approximatelyEqual(cooldown.getTimeSinceLastAttack(), 6.0f),
          "Cooldown continues increasing beyond threshold");
    cooldown.resetAttackTimer();
    check(!cooldown.canAttack(), "Reset makes attack unavailable again");
    check(approximatelyEqual(cooldown.getTimeSinceLastAttack(), 0.0f),
          "Reset sets cooldown timer to zero");
    cooldown.setState(EnemyState::FIGHTING);
    const float beforeFight = cooldown.getPosition().x;
    cooldown.update(2.0f);
    check(cooldown.canAttack(), "Cooldown runs during FIGHTING state");
    check(approximatelyEqual(cooldown.getPosition().x, beforeFight),
          "FIGHTING state stops movement");
    cooldown.setState(EnemyState::MOVING);
    cooldown.update(0.25f);
    check(approximatelyEqual(cooldown.getPosition().x, beforeFight - 15.0f),
          "Movement resumes and handles fractional deltaTime");

    std::cout << "\n--- Damage Boundaries and Invalid States ---\n";
    Enemy damageBoundary(7, 2, 110.0f, 1.0f, 1, glm::vec2(900.0f, 175.0f));
    damageBoundary.takeDamage(0);
    check(damageBoundary.getHealth() == 7, "Zero damage is ignored");
    damageBoundary.takeDamage(-1);
    check(damageBoundary.getHealth() == 7, "Negative damage is ignored");
    damageBoundary.takeDamage(6);
    check(damageBoundary.getHealth() == 1 &&
          damageBoundary.getState() != EnemyState::DEAD,
          "Damage leaving exactly 1 HP does not kill");
    damageBoundary.takeDamage(1);
    check(damageBoundary.getHealth() == 0 &&
          damageBoundary.getState() == EnemyState::DEAD,
          "Exact lethal damage clamps to zero and kills");
    damageBoundary.takeDamage(std::numeric_limits<int>::max());
    check(damageBoundary.getHealth() == 0,
          "Additional damage to dead enemy is ignored");
    damageBoundary.setState(EnemyState::FIGHTING);
    check(damageBoundary.getState() == EnemyState::DEAD,
          "Dead enemy cannot re-enter FIGHTING");
    damageBoundary.resetAttackTimer();
    check(!damageBoundary.canAttack(), "Dead enemy cannot attack after timer reset");

    Enemy livingState(5, 1, 60.0f, 2.0f, 0, glm::vec2(800.0f, 100.0f));
    livingState.setState(EnemyState::FIGHTING);
    livingState.setState(EnemyState::DEAD);
    check(livingState.getState() == EnemyState::FIGHTING,
          "setState(DEAD) cannot kill from FIGHTING without damage");
    livingState.setState(EnemyState::MOVING);
    check(livingState.getState() == EnemyState::MOVING,
          "Living enemy may return to MOVING");
    livingState.takeDamage(std::numeric_limits<int>::max());
    check(livingState.getHealth() == 0 &&
          livingState.getState() == EnemyState::DEAD,
          "Large overkill damage is clamped to zero");

    std::cout << "\n--- Config, Inheritance, and Ownership ---\n";
    EasyEnemy extraEasy(0, glm::vec2(900.0f, 100.0f), 0.75f);
    MediumEnemy extraMedium(1, glm::vec2(900.0f, 175.0f), 1.25f);
    HardEnemy extraHard(3, glm::vec2(900.0f, 325.0f), 3.0f);
    check(extraEasy.getHealth() == GameConfig::EASY_ENEMY_HP &&
          extraEasy.getDamage() == GameConfig::EASY_ENEMY_DAMAGE &&
          approximatelyEqual(extraEasy.getSpeed(), GameConfig::EASY_ENEMY_SPEED),
          "EasyEnemy initial stats follow GameConfig");
    check(extraMedium.getHealth() == GameConfig::MEDIUM_ENEMY_HP &&
          extraMedium.getDamage() == GameConfig::MEDIUM_ENEMY_DAMAGE &&
          approximatelyEqual(extraMedium.getSpeed(), GameConfig::MEDIUM_ENEMY_SPEED),
          "MediumEnemy initial stats follow GameConfig");
    check(extraHard.getHealth() == GameConfig::HARD_ENEMY_HP &&
          extraHard.getDamage() == GameConfig::HARD_ENEMY_DAMAGE &&
          approximatelyEqual(extraHard.getSpeed(), GameConfig::HARD_ENEMY_SPEED),
          "HardEnemy initial stats follow GameConfig");
    check(approximatelyEqual(extraEasy.getAttackInterval(), 0.75f) &&
          approximatelyEqual(extraMedium.getAttackInterval(), 1.25f) &&
          approximatelyEqual(extraHard.getAttackInterval(), 3.0f),
          "Each subclass retains its own supplied cooldown");
    check(extraEasy.getLane() == 0 && extraMedium.getLane() == 1 &&
          extraHard.getLane() == 3,
          "Subclass constructors preserve valid lane indices");

    std::unique_ptr<Enemy> polymorphic =
        std::make_unique<HardEnemy>(2, glm::vec2(900.0f, 250.0f), 2.0f);
    check(dynamic_cast<HardEnemy*>(polymorphic.get()) != nullptr,
          "Parent-class unique_ptr preserves the actual child type");
    polymorphic->update(1.0f);
    check(approximatelyEqual(polymorphic->getPosition().x,
                             900.0f - GameConfig::HARD_ENEMY_SPEED),
          "Parent-class unique_ptr can update subclass movement");
    polymorphic->takeDamage(GameConfig::HARD_ENEMY_HP);
    check(polymorphic->getState() == EnemyState::DEAD,
          "Parent-class unique_ptr can kill subclass enemy");

    struct DestructionProbe : Enemy {
        bool& wasDestroyed;
        explicit DestructionProbe(bool& flag)
            : Enemy(1, 1, 1.0f, 1.0f, 0, glm::vec2(0.0f)),
              wasDestroyed(flag) {}
        ~DestructionProbe() override { wasDestroyed = true; }
    };
    bool derivedDestructorCalled = false;
    {
        std::unique_ptr<Enemy> pointer =
            std::make_unique<DestructionProbe>(derivedDestructorCalled);
    }
    check(derivedDestructorCalled,
          "Virtual destructor calls derived destructor through Enemy pointer");

   
std::cout << "\n--- Negative deltaTime Regression ---\n";

Enemy negativeTime(5, 1, 60.0f, 2.0f, 0,
                   glm::vec2(900.0f, 100.0f));

float timerBeforeNegative = negativeTime.getTimeSinceLastAttack();

negativeTime.update(-1.0f);

check(approximatelyEqual(negativeTime.getPosition().x, 900.0f),
      "Negative deltaTime must not move enemy backwards");

check(approximatelyEqual(negativeTime.getTimeSinceLastAttack(),
                         timerBeforeNegative),
      "Negative deltaTime must not reduce attack timer");

    // -----------------------------------------
    // TEST: Virtual Attack Types
    // -----------------------------------------

    std::cout << "\n--- Enemy Attack Type Tests ---\n";

    EasyEnemy attackEasy(0, glm::vec2(900.0f, 100.0f), 2.0f);
    MediumEnemy attackMedium(1, glm::vec2(900.0f, 175.0f), 2.0f);
    HardEnemy attackHard(2, glm::vec2(900.0f, 250.0f), 2.0f);

    // Test direct subclass calls
    check(attackEasy.getAttackType() == AttackType::High,
          "EasyEnemy uses high projectile attacks");

    check(attackMedium.getAttackType() == AttackType::Low,
          "MediumEnemy uses low projectile attacks");

    check(attackHard.getAttackType() == AttackType::Melee,
          "HardEnemy uses melee attacks");

    // Test virtual dispatch through parent pointers
    const Enemy* easyPtr = &attackEasy;
    const Enemy* mediumPtr = &attackMedium;
    const Enemy* hardPtr = &attackHard;

    check(easyPtr->getAttackType() == AttackType::High,
          "EasyEnemy virtual dispatch works");

    check(mediumPtr->getAttackType() == AttackType::Low,
          "MediumEnemy virtual dispatch works");

    check(hardPtr->getAttackType() == AttackType::Melee,
          "HardEnemy virtual dispatch works");
      
    std::cout << "\n--- Immediate First Attack Tests ---\n";

    EasyEnemy firstAttackEnemy(
        0, glm::vec2(900.0f, 100.0f), 2.0f
    );

    check(firstAttackEnemy.canAttack(),
          "Enemy can attack immediately after spawning");

    firstAttackEnemy.resetAttackTimer();

    check(!firstAttackEnemy.canAttack(),
          "Enemy cannot attack immediately after reset");

    firstAttackEnemy.update(1.0f);

    check(!firstAttackEnemy.canAttack(),
          "Enemy must wait during cooldown");

    firstAttackEnemy.update(1.0f);

    check(firstAttackEnemy.canAttack(),
          "Enemy can attack again after cooldown");

    // -----------------------------------------
    // FINAL RESULTS
    // -----------------------------------------

    std::cout << "\n==============================\n";
    std::cout << "PASSED: " << passed << '\n';
    std::cout << "FAILED: " << failed << '\n';
    std::cout << "==============================\n";

    if (failed == 0) {
        std::cout << "ALL ENEMY TESTS PASSED!\n";
        return 0;
    }

    return 1;
}

========== tests/WaveManagerTest.cpp ==========
#include "../src/systems/WaveManager.h"
#include "../src/entities/EasyEnemy.h"
#include "../src/entities/MediumEnemy.h"
#include "../src/entities/HardEnemy.h"
#include "../src/utils/Constants.h"

#include <cmath>
#include <cstddef>
#include <iostream>
#include <limits>
#include <string>
#include <type_traits>
#include <utility>

namespace {

int passed = 0;
int failed = 0;

void check(bool condition, const std::string& description) {
    if (condition) {
        ++passed;
        std::cout << "[PASS] " << description << '\n';
    } else {
        ++failed;
        std::cerr << "[FAIL] " << description << '\n';
    }
}

bool near(float a, float b) {
    return std::fabs(a - b) < 0.001f;
}

bool samePosition(const glm::vec2& a, const glm::vec2& b) {
    return near(a.x, b.x) && near(a.y, b.y);
}

bool validLaneAndSpawn(const Enemy* enemy) {
    if (!enemy) return false;
    const int lane = enemy->getLane();
    return lane >= 0 && lane < GameConfig::LANE_COUNT &&
           near(enemy->getPosition().x, 900.0f) &&
           near(enemy->getPosition().y, 100.0f + lane * 75.0f);
}

bool isEasy(const Enemy* e) {
    return dynamic_cast<const EasyEnemy*>(e) != nullptr;
}
bool isMedium(const Enemy* e) {
    return dynamic_cast<const MediumEnemy*>(e) != nullptr;
}
bool isHard(const Enemy* e) {
    return dynamic_cast<const HardEnemy*>(e) != nullptr;
}

bool validTypeForWave(int wave, const Enemy* enemy) {
    if (!enemy) return false;
    if (wave == 1) return isEasy(enemy);
    if (wave == 2) return isEasy(enemy) || isMedium(enemy);
    if (wave == 3) return isEasy(enemy) || isMedium(enemy) || isHard(enemy);
    return false;
}

void defeatAll(WaveManager& manager) {
    // WaveManager owns the enemies; do not erase them while iterating.
    for (std::size_t i = 0; i < manager.getActiveEnemyCount(); ++i) {
        Enemy* enemy = manager.getEnemyAt(i);
        if (enemy) enemy->takeDamage(1000);
    }
}

void checkInitialState() {
    std::cout << "\n--- Initial state / getters ---\n";
    WaveManager manager;
    check(manager.getCurrentWave() == 1, "Starts at Wave 1");
    check(manager.getEnemiesSpawnedInWave() == 0, "Starts with zero spawned enemies");
    check(manager.getActiveEnemyCount() == 0, "Starts with an empty enemy vector");
    check(!manager.hasWon(), "Has not won initially");
    check(manager.getEnemyAt(0) == nullptr, "Index 0 is invalid when vector empty");
    check(manager.getEnemyAt(100) == nullptr, "Large index is invalid when vector empty");

    static_assert(
        std::is_same<decltype(std::declval<WaveManager&>().getEnemyAt(0)), Enemy*>::value,
        "Non-const WaveManager must provide mutable Enemy* getter");
    static_assert(
        std::is_same<decltype(std::declval<const WaveManager&>().getEnemyAt(0)), const Enemy*>::value,
        "Const WaveManager must provide const Enemy* getter");
    const WaveManager& readOnly = manager;
    check(readOnly.getEnemyAt(0) == nullptr, "Const getter handles invalid index");

    check(manager.getDropCount() == 0, "New game starts with zero collectibles");
    check(manager.getDropAt(0) == nullptr, "Empty drop list rejects index zero");
    check(manager.getDropAt(9999) == nullptr, "Empty drop list rejects large index");
    check(!manager.removeDropAt(0), "Cannot remove a drop from an empty list");
    check(readOnly.getDropAt(0) == nullptr, "Drop getter works on const WaveManager");
}

void checkTimeBoundaries() {
    std::cout << "\n--- Spawn timing / exact boundaries ---\n";
    WaveManager manager;
    manager.update(0.0f);
    check(manager.getEnemiesSpawnedInWave() == 0, "Zero time does not spawn");
    manager.update(2.5f);
    check(manager.getEnemiesSpawnedInWave() == 0, "2.5 seconds is below spawn interval");
    manager.update(-50.0f);
    check(manager.getEnemiesSpawnedInWave() == 0, "Negative time before spawn is ignored");
    manager.update(0.5f);
    check(manager.getEnemiesSpawnedInWave() == 1, "Exactly 3 seconds spawns first enemy");

    const Enemy* first = manager.getEnemyAt(0);
    check(isEasy(first), "Wave 1's first enemy is EasyEnemy");
    check(validLaneAndSpawn(first), "First enemy spawns in a valid lane and position");
    check(first && near(first->getTimeSinceLastAttack(), first->getAttackInterval()),
          "Newly spawned enemy's attack timer starts at its interval");
    check(first && first->canAttack(), "Newly spawned enemy can attack immediately");
    check(manager.getDropCount() == 0, "Spawning an enemy does not create a drop");

    manager.update(2.5f);
    check(manager.getEnemiesSpawnedInWave() == 1, "Another 2.5 seconds does not spawn early");
    manager.update(0.5f);
    check(manager.getEnemiesSpawnedInWave() == 2, "Second enemy spawns at 6 seconds");
    manager.update(3.0f);
    check(manager.getEnemiesSpawnedInWave() == 3, "Third enemy spawns at 9 seconds");
    manager.update(30.0f);
    check(manager.getEnemiesSpawnedInWave() == 3 && manager.getActiveEnemyCount() == 3,
          "Wave 1 never exceeds three enemies");
    check(manager.getCurrentWave() == 1, "Wave 1 does not advance while enemies are alive");
    check(manager.getEnemyAt(3) == nullptr, "Index exactly equal to size is invalid");
    check(manager.getEnemyAt(9999) == nullptr, "Very large index is invalid");
    check(manager.getDropCount() == 0, "Living enemies do not generate drops over time");

    WaveManager largeFrame;
    largeFrame.update(9.0f);
    check(largeFrame.getActiveEnemyCount() == 3,
          "One 9-second update spawns three enemies");
    bool allStartAtEdge = true;
    for (std::size_t i = 0; i < largeFrame.getActiveEnemyCount(); ++i) {
        allStartAtEdge &= validLaneAndSpawn(largeFrame.getEnemyAt(i));
    }
    check(allStartAtEdge, "All new enemies start at spawn edge in same large frame");
    largeFrame.update(0.0f);
    check(largeFrame.getActiveEnemyCount() == 3,
          "Zero-time update does not duplicate spawns");
}

void checkMovementAndStates() {
    std::cout << "\n--- Movement / negative delta / state ---\n";
    WaveManager manager;
    manager.update(3.0f);
    Enemy* enemy = manager.getEnemyAt(0);
    check(enemy != nullptr, "Can access existing enemy by mutable getter");
    if (!enemy) return;

    // An enemy starts ready for its first attack. Reset to test the next cooldown.
    enemy->resetAttackTimer();
    const float laneY = enemy->getPosition().y;
    manager.update(0.5f);
    check(near(enemy->getPosition().x, 870.0f),
          "EasyEnemy moves left 30 units in 0.5 s");
    check(near(enemy->getPosition().y, laneY), "Movement does not change lane Y");
    check(near(enemy->getTimeSinceLastAttack(), 0.5f),
          "WaveManager updates enemy attack timer");
    enemy->setState(EnemyState::FIGHTING);
    const float beforeFight = enemy->getPosition().x;
    manager.update(1.0f);
    check(near(enemy->getPosition().x, beforeFight), "FIGHTING enemy does not move");
    check(near(enemy->getTimeSinceLastAttack(), 1.5f),
          "FIGHTING enemy continues attack timer");
    enemy->setState(EnemyState::MOVING);
    manager.update(0.5f);
    check(near(enemy->getPosition().x, 840.0f), "MOVING state resumes movement");

    const float beforeNegative = enemy->getPosition().x;
    const float attackTimeBeforeNegative = enemy->getTimeSinceLastAttack();
    const int spawnedBeforeNegative = manager.getEnemiesSpawnedInWave();
    manager.update(-1.0f);
    check(near(enemy->getPosition().x, beforeNegative),
          "Negative WaveManager delta does not move an existing enemy");
    check(near(enemy->getTimeSinceLastAttack(), attackTimeBeforeNegative),
          "Negative WaveManager delta does not change cooldown");
    check(manager.getEnemiesSpawnedInWave() == spawnedBeforeNegative,
          "Negative WaveManager delta does not spawn an enemy");
}

void checkProgressionAndVictory() {
    std::cout << "\n--- Wave transitions, variety, victory ---\n";
    WaveManager manager;
    manager.update(6.0f);
    check(manager.getActiveEnemyCount() == 2 && manager.getCurrentWave() == 1,
          "Wave 1 contains two enemies after 6 seconds");

    defeatAll(manager);
    manager.update(0.0f);
    check(manager.getCurrentWave() == 1 && !manager.hasWon(),
          "Killing all spawned enemies early does not skip remaining spawn quota");
    check(manager.getDropCount() == 2,
          "First two Wave 1 deaths generate two drops before wave completion");

    manager.update(3.0f);
    check(manager.getCurrentWave() == 1 && manager.getActiveEnemyCount() == 3,
          "Final Wave 1 enemy still spawns after earlier kills");
    manager.update(0.0f);
    check(manager.getCurrentWave() == 1, "Wave 1 waits for final living enemy");
    if (Enemy* last = manager.getEnemyAt(2)) last->takeDamage(1000);
    manager.update(0.0f);
    check(manager.getCurrentWave() == 2, "Wave 1 advances to Wave 2 once all are dead");
    check(manager.getDropCount() == 3,
          "All three Wave 1 drops survive the Wave 1-to-2 transition");
    check(manager.getActiveEnemyCount() == 0, "Previous wave enemies are cleared");
    check(manager.getEnemyAt(0) == nullptr, "Old wave indices do not remain valid");
    check(manager.getEnemiesSpawnedInWave() == 0, "Wave 2 spawn counter resets");
    check(!manager.hasWon(), "No premature victory at start of Wave 2");

    manager.update(0.0f);
    check(manager.getActiveEnemyCount() == 0, "No immediate spawn upon transition");
    check(manager.getDropCount() == 3,
          "Zero-time update in Wave 2 preserves previous drops");
    manager.update(2.5f);
    check(manager.getActiveEnemyCount() == 0, "Wave 2 spawn timer starts fresh");
    manager.update(0.5f);
    check(manager.getActiveEnemyCount() == 1 && isEasy(manager.getEnemyAt(0)),
          "First Wave 2 enemy is guaranteed Easy");
    manager.update(12.0f);
    check(manager.getEnemiesSpawnedInWave() == GameConfig::WAVE_2_COUNT &&
          manager.getActiveEnemyCount() == GameConfig::WAVE_2_COUNT,
          "Wave 2 spawns exactly five enemies");
    check(isMedium(manager.getEnemyAt(1)), "Second Wave 2 enemy is guaranteed Medium");

    bool wave2TypesAndLanesOK = true;
    for (std::size_t i = 0; i < manager.getActiveEnemyCount(); ++i) {
        wave2TypesAndLanesOK &= validTypeForWave(2, manager.getEnemyAt(i));
        const Enemy* e = manager.getEnemyAt(i);
        if (!e || e->getLane() < 0 || e->getLane() >= GameConfig::LANE_COUNT ||
            !near(e->getPosition().y, 100.0f + e->getLane() * 75.0f)) {
            wave2TypesAndLanesOK = false;
        }
    }
    check(wave2TypesAndLanesOK, "Every Wave 2 enemy has an allowed type and valid lane");
    check(manager.getDropCount() == 3,
          "Spawning Wave 2 enemies does not duplicate Wave 1 drops");

    // Killing four of five must not advance the wave.
    for (std::size_t i = 0; i < 4; ++i) {
        if (Enemy* e = manager.getEnemyAt(i)) e->takeDamage(1000);
    }
    manager.update(0.0f);
    check(manager.getCurrentWave() == 2 && !manager.hasWon(),
          "Wave 2 does not advance with one enemy still alive");
    check(manager.getDropCount() == 7,
          "Four Wave 2 deaths add exactly four drops");
    if (Enemy* last = manager.getEnemyAt(4)) last->takeDamage(1000);
    manager.update(0.0f);
    check(manager.getCurrentWave() == 3 && manager.getActiveEnemyCount() == 0,
          "Wave 2 advances to Wave 3 only after last death");
    check(manager.getDropCount() == 8,
          "All eight drops from Waves 1 and 2 survive Wave 3 transition");
    check(manager.getEnemiesSpawnedInWave() == 0 && !manager.hasWon(),
          "Wave 3 counters reset and victory is false");

    // Spawn nine of ten enemies; verify all three types before killing them.
    manager.update(27.0f);
    check(manager.getEnemiesSpawnedInWave() == 9 && manager.getActiveEnemyCount() == 9,
          "Nine enemies spawn before Wave 3's tenth spawn interval");
    check(isEasy(manager.getEnemyAt(0)) && isMedium(manager.getEnemyAt(1)) &&
          isHard(manager.getEnemyAt(2)),
          "Wave 3 guarantees Easy, Medium, and Hard as first three enemies");

    bool wave3TypesAndLanesOK = true;
    for (std::size_t i = 0; i < manager.getActiveEnemyCount(); ++i) {
        const Enemy* e = manager.getEnemyAt(i);
        wave3TypesAndLanesOK &= validTypeForWave(3, e);
        if (!e || e->getLane() < 0 || e->getLane() >= GameConfig::LANE_COUNT ||
            !near(e->getPosition().y, 100.0f + e->getLane() * 75.0f)) {
            wave3TypesAndLanesOK = false;
        }
    }
    check(wave3TypesAndLanesOK, "Every Wave 3 enemy has a valid type and lane");

    // First three are guaranteed Easy, Medium, Hard: verify each death drops once.
    const std::size_t dropsBeforeThreeTypes = manager.getDropCount();
    for (std::size_t i = 0; i < 3; ++i) {
        if (Enemy* e = manager.getEnemyAt(i)) e->takeDamage(1000);
    }
    manager.update(0.0f);
    check(manager.getDropCount() == dropsBeforeThreeTypes + 3,
          "Easy, Medium, Hard each generate exactly one collectible");
    manager.update(0.0f);
    check(manager.getDropCount() == dropsBeforeThreeTypes + 3,
          "Repeated updates do not duplicate drops from any enemy type");
    check(manager.getCurrentWave() == 3 && !manager.hasWon(),
          "Killing only first three Wave 3 enemies does not end game");

    defeatAll(manager);
    manager.update(0.0f);
    check(!manager.hasWon() && manager.getCurrentWave() == 3,
          "No victory until all ten Wave 3 enemies have spawned");
    check(manager.getDropCount() == 17,
          "Nine Wave 3 deaths yield 17 cumulative collectibles");
    manager.update(3.0f);
    check(manager.getActiveEnemyCount() == GameConfig::WAVE_3_COUNT &&
          manager.getEnemiesSpawnedInWave() == GameConfig::WAVE_3_COUNT,
          "Tenth Wave 3 enemy spawns at 30 seconds");
    check(!manager.hasWon(), "Spawning tenth living enemy does not trigger victory");
    check(manager.getDropCount() == 17,
          "Tenth living enemy has not generated its drop");

    if (Enemy* last = manager.getEnemyAt(9)) last->takeDamage(1000);
    manager.update(0.0f);
    check(manager.hasWon(), "All spawned and defeated Wave 3 enemies trigger victory");
    check(manager.getDropCount() == 18,
          "Final enemy's collectible is retained when victory occurs");
    check(manager.getDropAt(17) != nullptr,
          "Final victory collectible is accessible by index");
    check(manager.getCurrentWave() == 3, "Game never advances to Wave 4");

    const std::size_t finalCount = manager.getActiveEnemyCount();
    const int finalSpawns = manager.getEnemiesSpawnedInWave();
    manager.update(100.0f);
    manager.update(0.0f);
    check(manager.hasWon() && manager.getCurrentWave() == 3,
          "Victory remains true after future updates");
    check(manager.getActiveEnemyCount() == finalCount &&
          manager.getEnemiesSpawnedInWave() == finalSpawns,
          "No extra enemies spawn after victory");
    check(manager.getDropCount() == 18,
          "Updates after victory cannot duplicate any of the 18 drops");
}

void checkRepeatedFreshManagers() {
    std::cout << "\n--- Repeated independent sessions ---\n";
    bool good = true;
    for (int run = 0; run < 8; ++run) {
        WaveManager manager;
        good &= manager.getCurrentWave() == 1 && !manager.hasWon();
        manager.update(9.0f);
        good &= manager.getActiveEnemyCount() == 3;
        defeatAll(manager);
        manager.update(0.0f);
        good &= manager.getCurrentWave() == 2;
        good &= manager.getDropCount() == 3;
        manager.update(15.0f);
        good &= manager.getActiveEnemyCount() == 5;
        good &= isEasy(manager.getEnemyAt(0)) && isMedium(manager.getEnemyAt(1));
        defeatAll(manager);
        manager.update(0.0f);
        good &= manager.getCurrentWave() == 3;
        good &= manager.getDropCount() == 8;
        manager.update(30.0f);
        good &= manager.getActiveEnemyCount() == 10;
        good &= isEasy(manager.getEnemyAt(0)) && isMedium(manager.getEnemyAt(1)) &&
                isHard(manager.getEnemyAt(2));
        defeatAll(manager);
        manager.update(0.0f);
        good &= manager.hasWon();
        good &= manager.getDropCount() == 18;
    }
    check(good, "Eight independent complete games have correct waves, drops, victory");
}

void checkSingleDropLifecycle() {
    std::cout << "\n--- Collectible creation / pickup / invalid indices ---\n";
    WaveManager manager;
    manager.update(GameConfig::ENEMY_SPAWN_INTERVAL);
    check(manager.getActiveEnemyCount() == 1,
          "One enemy spawned for collectible testing");
    check(manager.getDropCount() == 0,
          "Living enemy has not generated a collectible");

    Enemy* enemy = manager.getEnemyAt(0);
    if (!enemy) {
        check(false, "Spawned enemy is accessible for drop tests");
        return;
    }

    const glm::vec2 expectedPosition = enemy->getPosition();
    const int expectedLane = enemy->getLane();
    enemy->takeDamage(1000);
    check(manager.getDropCount() == 0,
          "Death is processed by WaveManager.update, not immediately by takeDamage");
    manager.update(0.0f);
    check(manager.getDropCount() == 1, "Dead enemy generates one collectible");

    const Collectible* drop = manager.getDropAt(0);
    check(drop && samePosition(drop->getPosition(), expectedPosition) &&
          drop->getLane() == expectedLane,
          "Collectible appears at dead enemy's position and lane");
    check(manager.getDropAt(1) == nullptr,
          "Index equal to drop count returns nullptr");
    check(manager.getDropAt(10) == nullptr,
          "Invalid collectible index returns nullptr");

    manager.update(0.0f);
    manager.update(0.0f);
    manager.update(0.5f);
    check(manager.getDropCount() == 1,
          "Repeated updates do not duplicate collectibles");

    const std::size_t enormous = std::numeric_limits<std::size_t>::max();
    check(manager.getDropAt(enormous) == nullptr,
          "Very large collectible index returns nullptr");
    check(!manager.removeDropAt(10), "Cannot remove nonexistent collectible");
    check(!manager.removeDropAt(enormous), "Very large removal index is rejected");
    check(manager.getDropCount() == 1,
          "Invalid removal does not change drop count");
    check(manager.removeDropAt(0), "Collectible is removed successfully");
    check(manager.getDropCount() == 0, "No collectibles remain after pickup");
    check(manager.getDropAt(0) == nullptr,
          "Collected drop cannot be accessed after removal");
    check(!manager.removeDropAt(0), "Same collectible cannot be collected twice");
    manager.update(0.0f);
    check(manager.getDropCount() == 0,
          "Already-collected drop is not regenerated by a dead enemy");
}

void checkMultipleDropsAndRemovalOrder() {
    std::cout << "\n--- Multiple deaths / removal order ---\n";
    WaveManager manager;
    manager.update(9.0f); // Wave 1: all three EasyEnemies spawn.
    check(manager.getActiveEnemyCount() == 3,
          "Three enemies available for multiple-drop test");
    if (manager.getActiveEnemyCount() != 3) return;

    const Enemy* first = manager.getEnemyAt(0);
    const Enemy* second = manager.getEnemyAt(1);
    const Enemy* third = manager.getEnemyAt(2);
    if (!first || !second || !third) {
        check(false, "All three enemies accessible for removal-order test");
        return;
    }
    const glm::vec2 firstPosition = first->getPosition();
    const glm::vec2 secondPosition = second->getPosition();
    const glm::vec2 thirdPosition = third->getPosition();
    const int firstLane = first->getLane();
    const int secondLane = second->getLane();
    const int thirdLane = third->getLane();

    // Process deaths one at a time so drop order is deterministic.
    manager.getEnemyAt(0)->takeDamage(1000);
    manager.update(0.0f);
    check(manager.getDropCount() == 1,
          "First enemy death produces first drop");
    check(manager.getCurrentWave() == 1,
          "Wave remains active while two enemies are alive");

    manager.getEnemyAt(1)->takeDamage(1000);
    manager.update(0.0f);
    check(manager.getDropCount() == 2,
          "Second enemy death produces second drop");

    manager.getEnemyAt(2)->takeDamage(1000);
    manager.update(0.0f);
    check(manager.getCurrentWave() == 2,
          "Wave advances when last of three enemies dies");
    check(manager.getDropCount() == 3,
          "Last enemy's drop survives when active enemies are cleared");

    const Collectible* drop0 = manager.getDropAt(0);
    const Collectible* drop1 = manager.getDropAt(1);
    const Collectible* drop2 = manager.getDropAt(2);
    check(drop0 && samePosition(drop0->getPosition(), firstPosition) &&
          drop0->getLane() == firstLane,
          "First drop preserves first enemy position and lane");
    check(drop1 && samePosition(drop1->getPosition(), secondPosition) &&
          drop1->getLane() == secondLane,
          "Second drop preserves second enemy position and lane");
    check(drop2 && samePosition(drop2->getPosition(), thirdPosition) &&
          drop2->getLane() == thirdLane,
          "Third drop preserves third enemy position and lane");

    check(manager.removeDropAt(1), "Removing middle drop succeeds");
    check(manager.getDropCount() == 2, "Middle removal reduces drop count to two");
    const Collectible* newFirst = manager.getDropAt(0);
    const Collectible* newSecond = manager.getDropAt(1);
    check(newFirst && samePosition(newFirst->getPosition(), firstPosition) &&
          newFirst->getLane() == firstLane,
          "Middle removal preserves the earlier drop");
    check(newSecond && samePosition(newSecond->getPosition(), thirdPosition) &&
          newSecond->getLane() == thirdLane,
          "Middle removal shifts the later drop into the removed index");
    check(manager.removeDropAt(1), "Removing last remaining index succeeds");
    check(manager.removeDropAt(0), "Removing first remaining index succeeds");
    check(manager.getDropCount() == 0, "Removing all drops leaves an empty list");
    manager.update(0.0f);
    check(manager.getDropCount() == 0,
          "Previously collected drops stay removed across a wave transition");
}

void checkNegativeTimeDefersDropProcessing() {
    std::cout << "\n--- Death processing boundaries ---\n";
    WaveManager manager;
    manager.update(3.0f);
    Enemy* enemy = manager.getEnemyAt(0);
    if (!enemy) {
        check(false, "Enemy available for negative-time drop test");
        return;
    }
    enemy->takeDamage(1000);
    manager.update(-1.0f);
    check(manager.getDropCount() == 0,
          "Negative WaveManager update does not process a pending death");
    manager.update(0.0f);
    check(manager.getDropCount() == 1,
          "Zero-time update processes a previously pending death");
    manager.update(0.0f);
    check(manager.getDropCount() == 1,
          "Pending death is recorded only once");
}

void checkDropIndependenceBetweenManagers() {
    std::cout << "\n--- Independent WaveManager drop storage ---\n";
    WaveManager managerA;
    WaveManager managerB;
    managerA.update(3.0f);
    managerB.update(3.0f);
    if (Enemy* e = managerA.getEnemyAt(0)) e->takeDamage(1000);
    managerA.update(0.0f);
    check(managerA.getDropCount() == 1,
          "First WaveManager records its enemy's drop");
    check(managerB.getDropCount() == 0,
          "Separate WaveManager has no drops from first manager");
    check(managerA.removeDropAt(0),
          "First WaveManager can remove its own drop");
    check(managerB.getDropCount() == 0,
          "Removing drops in first WaveManager does not affect another");
}

} // namespace

int main() {
    checkInitialState();
    checkTimeBoundaries();
    checkMovementAndStates();
    checkProgressionAndVictory();
    checkRepeatedFreshManagers();
    checkSingleDropLifecycle();
    checkMultipleDropsAndRemovalOrder();
    checkNegativeTimeDefersDropProcessing();
    checkDropIndependenceBetweenManagers();

    std::cout << "\n==============================\n";
    std::cout << "WaveManager tests: " << passed << " passed, "
              << failed << " failed\n";
    std::cout << "==============================\n";
    return failed == 0 ? 0 : 1;
}
